tbmm-2019-10-31sit01spe0008par0045	Mr. President, honorable lawmakers, I have made a commitment to speak off the record on the issues of the National Ministry of Education inspection system and its financial inspectors. I salute our Veteran Assembly. Respected MPs, the future of a nation depends on how it raises its new generation. Therefore, the 24th and 42nd articles of our Constitution have been decided that all educational activities in our country will be conducted under the supervision and supervision of the state. In our country, the control of all educational institutions is run by MEB financial inspectors. The MEB inspection system, which is such an important responsibility, has been modified four times in the last eight years, but has never been properly constructed. With the latest amendment in 2016, 475 inspectors selected by interview from investigators were appointed as Minister's financial inspectors, while the remaining investigators were left in the provinces of individual-related staffs. There have been dozens of mistrial cases filed against this interview, the case has been concluded at the Court of Prosecutors and has been decided to cancel with all the results of the interview, but the Ministry of National Education has not carried out this judicial decision, and the mystery of the Inspection Board remains. The Ministry of National Education employs 498 Ministry inspectors, whose legal status is discussed in the current situation. The ministry alone has around 60,000 official institutions and over a million employees. It is also impossible for around 80,000 institutions to be audited by 498 inspectors, given the specific educational institutions of the ministry. Clearly, where there is no control, there will be some violations of government rules. As a result, illegal classrooms, illegal courses, illegal studies, kindergartens, illegal student dorms continue to operate because there are not enough audits. In addition, the state's official educational institutions do not have adequate guidance and control. It's obvious that lack of control has severe consequences. Sadly, control is only remembered when bad things happen. Control only comes to mind that when our children die in a dorm in flames, when our children are abused in an illegal course, when our children are subjected to violence at a school, when a teacher is injured by a student, a little child is forgotten in the service and dies. Respectable lawmakers, it is unexplainable that only 498 of our professional investigators, who are in need of supervision from educational institutions, are employed and not covered by about 1,500 financial inspectors, to be kept in the municipal training departments with the individual staff. A failure in the MEB inspection system has been publicly established in the Court Control Report of 2018 of the Ministry of National Education, which states that 498 inspectors have not had sufficient inspections. The audit report specifically stressed that the inspection system must be restored to a stable structure and adequate capacity. It is clear that if the state neglects its command of control, it will have irreparable consequences for our country. As far as the issue is concerned, 1,500 financial inspectors, who are being held in the provinces with a personal staff, must be immediately transferred to the financial inspectorial staff, as well as their other colleagues.

tbmm-2019-10-31sit02spe0253par0336	Mr. President, I have made a personal commitment to the first chapter of the Law Proposal of Water Products Act. With this law proposal, which has been a work of over a year, which has been drawn together with all its shareholders and includes new regulations in many ways, the development of technological possibilities of Water Products Law 1380, which entered into force in 1971, aims to harmonise the present conditions, including scientific, environmental, economic and social issues and the needs of the sector. The proposal includes the need for sustainable production of water products and the protection of our fishing resources. Sector shareholders, academia, relevant NGOs, and true fishermen who follow the rules expect this proposal to be legalized as soon as possible. The Law Proposal for Changes in Water Products Law consists of 19 articles, including enforcement and executive clauses. With this proposal, the main aim is to harmonise the Water Products Law and the Law for Offenses, and to prevent illegal water products hunting, especially fish, and to deter illegal water products production activities. The licenses of fishing vessels are also available for a new arrangement. Under the new arrangement, administrative fines are brought to those who hunt by means of illegal boats, unregistered boats, games, trolls, algarnas, marine eggplants, and clams, who hunt by illegal means, and also the destruction of boats and hunting vehicles that are hand-held to all hunting vehicles and products they catch, including ships. If hunting activities such as licensed boats, games and trolls are carried out, such as zones, times, seasons, places, employment means should be carried out against the rules, such as genders and sizes, licenses for the first time in a month, if it is processed for the second time, a three-month suspension of licenses will be cancelled in the event of a third time in two years. Waterfields and water rentals are being determined by the powers of the relevant institutions, where the farming farms will be established by the Ministry of Environment and Urbanism and by the Ministry of Culture and Tourism. To release more fish to future generations, light fishing is banned primarily in the Sea of Marmara, Istanbul and Çanakkale Straits and in the Black Sea, using a net such as fun and trolls. Fishermen who apply this method are being fined for deterrent administrative fines and seized products. Water structures that allow water to migrate, procreation of water creatures in dams, regulators, and HESEs are organised by the opinion of universities, in order to ensure the continuation of biodiversity in areas with water structures on streams. In the same way, serious sanctions are brought on to those who pollut the streams or to those who do not give up the life water necessary for water creatures to survive. In order to prevent the spread of natural species in our water supply and alien species, fishing activities are subject to the rule in order to protect our water resources. In our natural lakes, our streams, even our seas, there is an administrative fine imposed on those who leave alien species without permission, such as piranhas, Americans, or European sea bass. In fact, this arrangement aims to boost awareness and deterrence. A new substance allows water products, hunting and breeding activities to be monitored, monitored, and recorded by remote detection systems and technological possibilities. So fish fishing activities and farming activities on farms can be monitored by institutions by technological possibilities. Those who act against the rules will receive an electronic fine. Under a new arrangement, water products are ordered to be smuggled abroad and to be kept alive in order to protect the creatures of our country's natural resources. Especially those who smuggle our native native species abroad by illegal means, such as sea eggplants and aquivades, which are low in consumption in our country, even leeches, are fined with serious administrative fines. In addition, the seizure of transport and hunting vehicles used for this purpose is organised, and in the case of a repeat violation, a year and three years in prison is served.
